\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{first\_zones\_zonelist()}{first\_zones\_zonelist()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{mmzone_8h_a4f00871647d56620ed3928e0b7f88e99} 
struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structzoneref}{zoneref}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} first\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structzonelist}{zonelist}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{zonelist}{, }\item[{enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{mmzone_8h_a0fdf79ffb0b79648e58d693137ccfada}{zone\+\_\+type}}}]{highest\+\_\+zoneidx}{, }\item[{nodemask\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{nodes}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

first\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist -\/ Returns the first zone at or below highest\+\_\+zoneidx within the allowed nodemask in a zonelist @zonelist:\+ The zonelist to search for a suitable zone @highest\+\_\+zoneidx:\+ The zone index of the highest zone to return @nodes:\+ An optional nodemask to filter the zonelist with

This function returns the first zone at or below a given zone index that is within the allowed nodemask. The zoneref returned is a cursor that can be used to iterate the zonelist with next\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist by advancing it by one before calling.

When no eligible zone is found, zoneref-\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}zone is NULL (zoneref itself is never NULL). This may happen either genuinely, or due to concurrent nodemask update due to cpuset modification.

Return:\+ Zoneref pointer for the first suitable zone found

\+\_\+\+\_\+always\+\_\+inline struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structzoneref}{zoneref}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} next\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \doxymbox{\hyperlink{structzoneref}{zoneref}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{z}{, }\item[{enum \doxymbox{\hyperlink{mmzone_8h_a0fdf79ffb0b79648e58d693137ccfada}{zone\+\_\+type}}}]{highest\+\_\+zoneidx}{, }\item[{nodemask\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{nodes}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

next\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist -\/ Returns the next zone at or below highest\+\_\+zoneidx within the allowed nodemask using a cursor within a zonelist as a starting point @z:\+ The cursor used as a starting point for the search @highest\+\_\+zoneidx:\+ The zone index of the highest zone to return @nodes:\+ An optional nodemask to filter the zonelist with

This function returns the next zone at or below a given zone index that is within the allowed nodemask using a cursor as the starting point for the search. The zoneref returned is a cursor that represents the current zone being examined. It should be advanced by one before calling next\+\_\+zones\+\_\+zonelist again.

Return:\+ the next zone at or below highest\+\_\+zoneidx within the allowed nodemask using a cursor within a zonelist as a starting point
